## asparaġni (n-asparaġni)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: asparagus
    **MT**: Pjanta li z-zokk tagħha jittiekel bħala ħaxix wara li jissaħħan.
- **Alternative Forms**: spraġ
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Is-sajjied xeraq l-asparaġni mal-ħaxix fit-tisjir tal-ikla.
    EN: The cook prepared asparagus with vegetables for the meal.

## assoġġetta (v-assoġġetta)
- **POS**: verb
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to subject, to subdue
    **MT**: Azzjoni jew azzjonijiet ta' to subject, to subdue.
- **Alternative Forms**: ssuġġetta
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ir-re assoġġetta l-poplu kollu għal-liġijiet it-ġodda tiegħu.
    EN: The king subjected the entire population to his new laws.

## distorbju (n-distorbju)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: noise, din
    **MT**: Ħoss qawwi u skomdu li jfixkel il-kwiet u r-ras.
- **Alternative Forms**: storbju
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-użu ta' distorbju kien evidenti f'dan il-każ.

## iskiet (n-iskiet)
- **POS**: noun
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: silence
    **MT**: L-istat li ma jkun hemm l-ebda ħoss jew storbju biex tkun fil-kwiet.
- **Alternative Forms**: skiet
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Wara l-mewt ta' nannuh, kien hemm iskiet kbir fil-dar.
    EN: After his grandfather's death, there was great silence in the house.

- **Root**: s-w-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: correctly
    **MT**: B'mod jew fi stil li jikkorrispondi għal correctly.
  - **EN**: very much
    **MT**: B'mod jew fi stil li jikkorrispondi għal very much.
  - **EN**: precisely
    **MT**: B'mod jew fi stil li jikkorrispondi għal precisely.
  - **EN**: just, only
    **MT**: B'mod jew fi stil li jikkorrispondi għal just, only.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Għamel xogħlu b'mod sewwa u rċieva tifħir kbir mingħand il-poplu.
    EN: He did his work correctly and received great praise from the people.

---

## sewwa (n-sewwa)
- **POS**: noun
- **Gender**: masculine
- **Root**: s-w-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: truth
    **MT**: Klassi jew terminu li jfisser truth.
  - **EN**: that which is true and just
    **MT**: Klassi jew terminu li jfisser that which is true and just.
  - **EN**: the right thing
    **MT**: L-att jew il-proċess ta' the right th.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: L-istudju u l-preżenza ta' sewwa kienu importanti.
    EN: The study and presence of truth were important.

---

## sewwa (v-sewwa)
- **POS**: verb
- **Root**: s-w-j
- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to repair, fix
    **MT**: Aġġusta u sewwa oġġett li kien miksur.
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Il-mekkanik sewwa l-magna f'qasir żmien.
    EN: The mechanic repaired the engine in a short time.

---

    EN: He performed ( transitive ) to curse, to imprecate at every moment.

- **Definitions**:
  - **EN**: to stand, to endure, to tolerate
    **MT**: Azzjoni jew azzjonijiet ta' to stand, to endure, to tolerate.
- **Alternative Forms**: staporta
- **Usage Examples**:
  - MT: Ssaporta r-responsabbiltajiet tiegħu bil-għaqal u l-impenn.
    EN: He performed to stand, to endure, to tolerate responsibly and with commitment.

---
